vulkan_dedicated_allocation_threshold
If true, the main thread pauses and unpauses the Vulkan worker threads around each texture deallocation, which allows the workers to make a little bit of progress but results in main thread stalls. If false, we keep the workers paused until all deallocations are done, allowing the deallocations to complete significantly faster.
